{/* Generated by scripts/docs-components.mjs. Do not edit. */}

Each component below is drawn with the Pretex[t] brand kit's style. Copy the React file and its CSS into your project. The look is in the custom properties at the top of the CSS.

**Accessibility:** 71 of 73 components pass an automated scan with axe-core 4.14.0 against WCAG 2.2 AA. Each component is scanned in a browser as drawn here, at rest. Hover and focus states, keyboard use and screen reader output still need a person to check.

## Button · Primary

<PreviewButtonPrimary />

<Check>
**Accessibility: no issues found.** axe-core 4.14.0 passed 4 WCAG 2.2 AA checks on this component.
</Check>

<CodeGroup>
```tsx ButtonPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./ButtonPrimary.css";

interface ButtonPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  onClick?: () => void;
}

/** Primary button, from the brand kit. */
export function ButtonPrimary({ className, children = "Primary", onClick }: ButtonPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <button className={className ? `button-primary ${className}` : "button-primary"} type="button" onClick={onClick}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}
```

```css ButtonPrimary.css
.button-primary {
  --button-primary-background: var(--kit-hz1nhj, #00ab8c);
  --button-primary-color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402);
  --button-primary-font-family: "General Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --button-primary-font-size: 15px;
  --button-primary-font-weight: 500;
  --button-primary-line-height: 1;
  --button-primary-padding: 12px 16px;
  --button-primary-radius: 8px;
  --button-primary-border: 1px solid var(--kit-hz1nhj, #00ab8c);
  --button-primary-border-top-width: 1px;
  --button-primary-border-left-width: 1px;
  --button-primary-border-right-width: 1px;
  --button-primary-border-bottom-width: 1px;
  --button-primary-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px color-mix(in srgb, var(--kit-hz1nhj, #00ab8c) 48%, transparent);
  --button-primary-icon-size: 17px;
  --button-primary-hover-background: #2ea68b;
  --button-primary-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --button-primary-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--button-primary-background);
  color: var(--button-primary-color);
  font-family: var(--button-primary-font-family);
  font-size: var(--button-primary-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--button-primary-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--button-primary-line-height);
  padding: var(--button-primary-padding);
  border-radius: var(--button-primary-radius);
  border: var(--button-primary-border);
  border-top-width: var(--button-primary-border-top-width);
  border-left-width: var(--button-primary-border-left-width);
  border-right-width: var(--button-primary-border-right-width);
  border-bottom-width: var(--button-primary-border-bottom-width);
  box-shadow: var(--button-primary-shadow);
}

.button-primary { cursor: pointer; }
.button-primary:hover {
  background: var(--button-primary-hover-background, var(--button-primary-background));
  color: var(--button-primary-hover-color, var(--button-primary-color));
  border-color: var(--button-primary-hover-border-color, currentColor);
  box-shadow: var(--button-primary-hover-shadow, var(--button-primary-shadow));
}
.button-primary:focus-visible {
  outline: var(--button-primary-focus-ring-width, 2px) solid var(--button-primary-focus-ring-color, currentColor);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}
```
</CodeGroup>

## Button · Ghost Dark

<Warning>
**Accessibility: 1 issue** found by axe-core 4.14.0 (WCAG 2.2 AA).

- **Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds** (serious): 1.06:1, needs 4.5:1 (#faf8f5 on #ffffff). [About this rule](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.14/color-contrast)

```css CardStudyResults.css
  --card-study-results-body-size: 14px;
  --card-study-results-title-weight: 600;
  --card-study-results-icon-color: #486dca;
  --card-study-results-icon-size: 20px;
  --card-study-results-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --card-study-results-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--card-study-results-background);
  color: var(--card-study-results-color);
  font-family: var(--card-study-results-font-family);
  font-size: var(--card-study-results-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--card-study-results-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--card-study-results-line-height);
  padding: var(--card-study-results-padding);
  border-radius: var(--card-study-results-radius);
  border: var(--card-study-results-border);
  border-top-width: var(--card-study-results-border-top-width);
  border-left-width: var(--card-study-results-border-left-width);
  border-right-width: var(--card-study-results-border-right-width);
  border-bottom-width: var(--card-study-results-border-bottom-width);
  box-shadow: var(--card-study-results-shadow);
}

.card-study-results { display: grid; gap: 6px; align-content: start; width: 100%; min-width: 0;  }
.card-study-results:hover {
  background: var(--card-study-results-hover-background, var(--card-study-results-background));
  color: var(--card-study-results-hover-color, var(--card-study-results-color));
  border-color: var(--card-study-results-hover-border-color, currentColor);
  box-shadow: var(--card-study-results-hover-shadow, var(--card-study-results-shadow));
}
.card-study-results__text { display: grid; gap: 6px; align-content: start; }
.card-study-results__icon { color: var(--card-study-results-icon-color, currentColor); }
.card-study-results__title { font-weight: var(--card-study-results-title-weight, bolder); }
.card-study-results__body { width: 100%; margin: 0; white-space: pre-line; opacity: 0.8; font-size: var(--card-study-results-body-size, inherit); }
.card-study-results__slots { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.card-study-results__slots:not([data-dividers]) { gap: 12px; }
.card-study-results__slots[data-dividers] > .card-study-results__slot + .card-study-results__slot { border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 14%, transparent); }
.card-study-results__slot, .card-study-results__items { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.card-study-results__items { align-items: flex-start; }
.card-study-results__slot[data-led] { flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
.card-study-results__slot[data-led] > .card-study-results__items { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.card-study-results__items[data-layout="row"] { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; }
.card-study-results__items[data-layout="grid"] { display: grid; }
.card-study-results__items[data-fill] > * { align-self: stretch; width: auto; min-width: 0; }
.card-study-results__items[data-fill][data-layout="row"] > :not([data-flex-item]) { flex: 1 1 0; }
.card-study-results__slot[data-led] > .card-study-results__items[data-fill] { flex: 1 1 0; }
.card-study-results__lead { flex: 1 1 8rem; min-width: 0; }
```

```tsx Dialog.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./Dialog.css";

interface DialogProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/** Dialog dialog, from the brand kit. */
export function Dialog({ className, children = "Dialog" }: DialogProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className ? `dialog ${className}` : "dialog"} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Dialog">
      <strong className="dialog__title">{children}</strong>
      <p className="dialog__body">Supporting details and a clear next step.</p>
      <div className="dialog__actions">
        <button type="button">Cancel</button>
        <button type="button">Confirm</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css Dialog.css
  border-left-width: var(--dialog-border-left-width);
  border-right-width: var(--dialog-border-right-width);
  border-bottom-width: var(--dialog-border-bottom-width);
  box-shadow: var(--dialog-shadow);
}

.dialog { display: grid; gap: 12px; width: min(100%, 24rem); }
.dialog { box-shadow: 0 12px 32px color-mix(in srgb, #000 20%, transparent); }
.dialog__body { margin: 0; }
.dialog__actions { display: flex; gap: 8px; justify-content: end; }
```

```tsx MenuDropdownMenu.tsx
import type * as React from "react";

interface MenuDropdownMenuProps {

  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/** Dropdown Menu menu, from the brand kit. */
export function MenuDropdownMenu({ className, children = "Dropdown Menu" }: MenuDropdownMenuProps) {
  return (
    <details className={className ? `menu-dropdown-menu ${className}` : "menu-dropdown-menu"}>
      <summary className="menu-dropdown-menu__trigger">{children}
        <svg className="menu-dropdown-menu__chevron" width={14} height={14} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        </svg>
      </summary>
      <div className="menu-dropdown-menu__list" role="menu">
        <button className="menu-dropdown-menu__item" role="menuitem" type="button">Rename</button>
        <button className="menu-dropdown-menu__item" role="menuitem" type="button">Duplicate</button>
        <button className="menu-dropdown-menu__item" role="menuitem" type="button">Delete</button>
      </div>
    </details>
  );
}
```

```css MenuDropdownMenu.css
.menu-dropdown-menu__item { all: unset; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: calc(var(--menu-dropdown-menu-radius) - 2px); cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap; }
```

## Select · Primary

```tsx SelectPrimary.tsx
import { Select } from "@base-ui/react/select";
import "./SelectPrimary.css";

interface SelectPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  onValueChange?: (value: string | null) => void;
  options?: string[];
  placeholder?: string;
  value?: string | null;
}

/** SelectPrimary: requires @base-ui/react for its accessible dropdown. */
export function SelectPrimary({ className, onValueChange, options = ["Overview","Settings","Activity"], placeholder = "Primary", value }: SelectPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <Select.Root defaultValue={placeholder ? null : "Choose an option"} value={value} onValueChange={onValueChange}>
      <div className={className ? `select-primary ${className}` : "select-primary"}>
        <Select.Trigger className="select-primary__trigger" aria-label={placeholder || "Choose an option"}>
          <Select.Value placeholder={placeholder} />
          <Select.Icon><svg className="select-primary__chevron" width={14} height={14} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
  <path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
</svg></Select.Icon>
        </Select.Trigger>
      </div>
      <Select.Portal>
        <Select.Positioner className="select-primary__positioner" sideOffset={4} alignItemWithTrigger={false}>
          <Select.Popup className="select-primary__list">
            <Select.List>
              {options.map((option) => (
                <Select.Item className="select-primary__item" key={option} value={option}>
                  <Select.ItemText>{option}</Select.ItemText>
                </Select.Item>
              ))}
            </Select.List>
          </Select.Popup>
        </Select.Positioner>
      </Select.Portal>
    </Select.Root>
  );
}
```

## Tabs · Primary

<PreviewTabsPrimary />

<Check>
**Accessibility: no issues found.** axe-core 4.14.0 passed 10 WCAG 2.2 AA checks on this component.
</Check>

<CodeGroup>
```tsx TabsPrimary.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import "./TabsPrimary.css";

interface TabsPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  onChange?: (tab: string) => void;
  tabs?: string[];
  value?: string;
}

/** Tabs from the brand kit: the open one is marked, and a click moves it. */
export function TabsPrimary({
  className,
  onChange,
  tabs = ["Overview","Settings","Activity"],
  value = "Overview",
}: TabsPrimaryProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(value);
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Tabs" className={className ? `tabs-primary ${className}` : "tabs-primary"}>
      {tabs.map((tab) => (
        <button
          aria-current={tab === open ? "page" : undefined}
          className="tabs-primary__tab"
          key={tab}
          onClick={() => {
            setOpen(tab);
            onChange?.(tab);
          }}
          type="button"
        >
          {tab}
        </button>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```


```css TablePrimary.css
.table-primary {
  --table-primary-background: var(--kit-hqrhrk, #f2f0ed);
  --table-primary-color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402);
  --table-primary-font-family: "General Sans", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --table-primary-font-size: 16px;
  --table-primary-font-weight: 500;
  --table-primary-line-height: 1.25;
  --table-primary-padding: 0;
  --table-primary-radius: 8px;
  --table-primary-border: 1px solid var(--kit-yr89bp, #e3e1de);
  --table-primary-border-top-width: 1px;
  --table-primary-border-left-width: 1px;
  --table-primary-border-right-width: 1px;
  --table-primary-border-bottom-width: 1px;
  --table-primary-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 3px color-mix(in srgb, #000000 7%, transparent);
  --table-primary-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --table-primary-focus-ring-width: 2px;
  --table-primary-head-background: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402);
  --table-primary-head-color: var(--kit-xoaop8, #faf8f5);

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--table-primary-background);
  color: var(--table-primary-color);
  font-family: var(--table-primary-font-family);
  font-size: var(--table-primary-font-size);
  font-weight: var(--table-primary-font-weight);
  line-height: var(--table-primary-line-height);
  padding: var(--table-primary-padding);
  border-radius: var(--table-primary-radius);
  border: var(--table-primary-border);
  border-top-width: var(--table-primary-border-top-width);
  border-left-width: var(--table-primary-border-left-width);
  border-right-width: var(--table-primary-border-right-width);
  border-bottom-width: var(--table-primary-border-bottom-width);
  box-shadow: var(--table-primary-shadow);
}

.table-primary { width: 100%; border-spacing: 0; border-collapse: separate; padding: 0; contain: paint; }
.table-primary__item-label { display: inline-flex; gap: 0.35em; align-items: center; }
.table-primary__item-label svg { flex: none; width: 1em; height: 1em; }
.table-primary th, .table-primary td { padding: 8px 12px; vertical-align: middle; text-align: start; }
.table-primary th { font-size: 0.85em; font-weight: 600; color: var(--table-primary-head-color, color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 65%, transparent)); }
.table-primary thead { background: var(--table-primary-head-background, transparent); }
.table-primary__stack { display: grid; gap: 0.15em; }
.table-primary__stack > small { font-size: 0.85em; color: color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 72%, transparent); }
.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; padding: 0.15em 0.6em; font-size: 0.8em; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1.4; color: var(--table-primary-tint); white-space: nowrap; border-radius: 999px; }
.table-primary__tag { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--table-primary-tint) 16%, transparent); }
.table-primary__chip { border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--table-primary-tint) 40%, transparent); }
.table-primary__chips { display: inline-flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.35em; }
.table-primary__button { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; padding: 0.35em 0.9em; font: inherit; font-size: 0.85em; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1.3; white-space: nowrap; cursor: pointer; border: 1px solid var(--table-primary-tint); border-radius: 0.4em; }
.table-primary__button[data-style="solid"] { color: #fff; background: var(--table-primary-tint); }
.table-primary__button[data-style="outline"] { color: var(--table-primary-tint); background: transparent; }
.table-primary__progress { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.35em; width: 100%; min-inline-size: 8rem; }
.table-primary__progress small { font-size: inherit; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.table-primary__progress-head { display: flex; gap: 1em; justify-content: space-between; }
.table-primary__bar { display: block; block-size: 6px; overflow: hidden; background: color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 12%, transparent); border-radius: 999px; }
.table-primary__bar > span { display: block; block-size: 100%; background: var(--table-primary-tint); border-radius: inherit; }
.table-primary th, .table-primary td { border-bottom: 1px solid var(--kit-yr89bp, #e3e1de); }
.table-primary td:nth-child(1) { --table-primary-tint: #022732; }
.table-primary td:nth-child(1) { color: #022732; }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(1) > td:nth-child(2) { --table-primary-tint: var(--kit-uv4dhm, #006733); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(1) > td:nth-child(2) .table-primary__item-label svg { width: 13px; height: 13px; color: var(--kit-uv4dhm, #006733); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(1) > td:nth-child(2) :is(.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip, .table-primary__button, .table-primary__progress) { background: #ffffff00; border: 2px solid var(--kit-uv4dhm, #006733); border-radius: 8px; color: var(--kit-uv4dhm, #006733); font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; line-height: 1; padding-block: 4px; padding-inline: 10px; }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(2) { --table-primary-tint: var(--kit-13autsx, #636861); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(2) .table-primary__item-label svg { width: 13px; height: 13px; color: var(--kit-13autsx, #636861); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(2) :is(.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip, .table-primary__button, .table-primary__progress) { background: #ffffff00; border: 2px solid var(--kit-yr89bp, #e3e1de); border-radius: 8px; color: var(--kit-13autsx, #636861); font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; line-height: 1; padding-block: 4px; padding-inline: 10px; }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(1) > td:nth-child(3) { --table-primary-tint: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(1) > td:nth-child(3) .table-primary__item-label svg { width: 14px; height: 14px; color: var(--kit-uv4dhm, #006733); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(1) > td:nth-child(3) :is(.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip, .table-primary__button, .table-primary__progress) { background: transparent; border: 1px solid transparent; border-radius: 0px; color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402); font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; line-height: 1.25; padding-block: 0px; padding-inline: 0px; }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(3) { --table-primary-tint: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(3) .table-primary__item-label svg { width: 13px; height: 13px; color: var(--kit-uv4dhm, #006733); }
.table-primary tbody > tr:nth-child(2) > td:nth-child(3) :is(.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip, .table-primary__button, .table-primary__progress) { background: transparent; border: 1px solid transparent; border-radius: 0px; color: var(--kit-18u0jwu, #020402); font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; line-height: 1.25; padding-block: 0px; padding-inline: 0px; }
.table-primary td:nth-child(4) { --table-primary-tint: var(--kit-hnp52d, #2a50c0); }
.table-primary td:nth-child(4) :is(.table-primary__tag, .table-primary__chip, .table-primary__button, .table-primary__progress) { background: transparent; border: none; border-radius: 0px; color: var(--kit-hnp52d, #2a50c0); font-size: 15px; font-weight: 600; line-height: 1; padding-block: 0px; padding-inline: 0px; }
.table-primary > tbody > tr:last-child > td { border-block-end: 0 !important; }
```

```tsx ToastPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./ToastPrimary.css";

interface ToastPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/** Primary toast, from the brand kit. */
export function ToastPrimary({ className, children = "Primary" }: ToastPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className ? `toast-primary ${className}` : "toast-primary"} role="status">
      <span className="toast-primary__text">{children}</span>
      <button className="toast-primary__action" type="button">Undo</button>
      <button className="toast-primary__close" type="button" aria-label="Close">
        <svg width="1em" height="1em" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M18 6 6 18M6 6l12 12" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css ToastPrimary.css
.toast-primary__text { flex: 1; min-width: 0; }
```

```tsx PanelPrimary.tsx
import { Collapsible } from "@base-ui/react/collapsible";
import "./PanelPrimary.css";

type PanelPrimaryProps = Pick<Collapsible.Root.Props, "open" | "defaultOpen" | "onOpenChange"> & { className?: string; toggleSide?: "left" | "right" };

export function PanelPrimary({ className, toggleSide = "right", defaultOpen = true, ...props }: PanelPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <Collapsible.Root {...props} defaultOpen={defaultOpen} data-toggle-side={toggleSide} aria-label="Primary" className={["panel-primary", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}>
      <Collapsible.Panel className="panel-primary__inner">
        <div className="panel-primary__surface">
<div className="panel-primary__slots" data-dividers>
  <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "16px 24px" }}>
    <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
    <strong className="panel-primary__lead panel-primary__heading">Primary</strong>
      <span className="panel-primary__header-1">
        <svg className="panel-primary__header-1__icon" width={9} height={9} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M1.25 12C1.25 6.06294 6.06294 1.25 12 1.25C17.9371 1.25 22.75 6.06294 22.75 12C22.75 17.9371 17.9371 22.75 12 22.75C6.06294 22.75 1.25 17.9371 1.25 12Z" fill="currentColor" clipRule="evenodd" fillRule="evenodd" strokeWidth="2" />
        </svg>
        <span>3</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 12, padding: "24px 24px" }}>
    <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 12 }}>
      <div className="panel-primary__body-1">
        <span className="panel-primary__body-1__label">
          <span>Review progress</span>
          <span>60%</span>
        </span>
        <span className="panel-primary__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
          <span className="panel-primary__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "20px 24px" }}>
    <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" data-fill style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "stretch" }}>
      <button className="panel-primary__footer-1" type="button">
        + New item
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>
      </Collapsible.Panel>
      <div className="panel-primary__hide">
<Collapsible.Trigger className="panel-primary__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Primary" /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-primary__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
  <svg className="panel-primary__hide-1__icon" width={17} height={17} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
    <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
  </svg>
</button></div>
</div>
    </Collapsible.Root>
  );
}
```

```css PanelPrimary.css
.panel-primary {

  --panel-primary-padding: 0px 1px;
  --panel-primary-radius: 8px;

  --panel-primary-border-top-width: 1px;

  --panel-primary-focus-ring-color: currentColor;
  --panel-primary-focus-ring-width: 2px;

  box-sizing: border-box;
  background: var(--panel-primary-background);

}

.panel-primary { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; width: 100%; min-width: 0; background: transparent; border: 0; padding: 0; box-shadow: none; }

.panel-primary[data-toggle-side="left"] { flex-direction: row-reverse; }
.panel-primary[data-toggle-side="left"] > .panel-primary__hide :is(svg:first-of-type, .panel-primary__chevron) { scale: -1 1; }
.panel-primary__slot[data-slot="header"] { background-color: var(--panel-primary-header-background, transparent); }
.panel-primary__heading { color: var(--panel-primary-header-color, inherit); font-family: var(--panel-primary-header-font-family, inherit); font-size: var(--panel-primary-header-font-size, inherit); font-weight: var(--panel-primary-header-font-weight, bolder); line-height: var(--panel-primary-header-line-height, inherit); }
.panel-primary__surface { box-sizing: border-box; width: 100%; min-width: 0; background: var(--panel-primary-background); padding: var(--panel-primary-padding); border: var(--panel-primary-border); border-radius: var(--panel-primary-radius); box-shadow: var(--panel-primary-shadow); }
.panel-primary__inner { width: 100%; min-width: 0; transition: width 260ms ease, overflow 0s 260ms allow-discrete; }
.panel-primary__inner[data-starting-style], .panel-primary__inner[data-ending-style], .panel-primary__inner[data-panel-open="false"] { width: 0; overflow: clip; transition-delay: 0s; }
.panel-primary__hide { display: flex; position: relative; flex: none; }
.panel-primary__hide > [inert] { display: flex; }
.panel-primary__trigger { position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: 1; background: transparent; border: 0; cursor: pointer; }
.panel-primary__trigger[aria-expanded="false"] + [inert] svg:first-of-type, .panel-primary__trigger[aria-expanded="false"] + [inert] .panel-primary__chevron { rotate: 180deg; }
.panel-primary__trigger:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid currentColor; outline-offset: 2px; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .panel-primary__inner { transition: none; } }
.panel-primary__slots { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.panel-primary__slots:not([data-dividers]) { gap: 12px; }
.panel-primary__slots[data-dividers] > .panel-primary__slot + .panel-primary__slot { border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 14%, transparent); }
.panel-primary__slot, .panel-primary__items { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.panel-primary__items { align-items: flex-start; }
.panel-primary__slot[data-led] { flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
.panel-primary__slot[data-led] > .panel-primary__items { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.panel-primary__items[data-layout="row"] { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; }
.panel-primary__items[data-layout="grid"] { display: grid; }
.panel-primary__items[data-fill] > * { align-self: stretch; width: auto; min-width: 0; }
.panel-primary__items[data-fill][data-layout="row"] > :not([data-flex-item]) { flex: 1 1 0; }
.panel-primary__slot[data-led] > .panel-primary__items[data-fill] { flex: 1 1 0; }
.panel-primary__lead { flex: 1 1 8rem; min-width: 0; }
.panel-primary__items:not([data-layout="row"]) > .panel-primary__lead { flex: none; }

.panel-primary__header-1 {
```

```tsx PanelWorkspacePanel.tsx
import { Collapsible } from "@base-ui/react/collapsible";
import "./PanelWorkspacePanel.css";

type PanelWorkspacePanelProps = Pick<Collapsible.Root.Props, "open" | "defaultOpen" | "onOpenChange"> & { className?: string; toggleSide?: "left" | "right" };

export function PanelWorkspacePanel({ className, toggleSide = "left", defaultOpen = true, ...props }: PanelWorkspacePanelProps) {
  return (
    <Collapsible.Root {...props} defaultOpen={defaultOpen} data-toggle-side={toggleSide} aria-label="Workspace Panel" className={["panel-workspace-panel", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}>
      <Collapsible.Panel className="panel-workspace-panel__inner">
        <div className="panel-workspace-panel__surface">
<div className="panel-workspace-panel__slots">
  <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "12px 24px" }}>
    <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
    <strong className="panel-workspace-panel__lead panel-workspace-panel__heading">Workspace Panel</strong>
      <span className="panel-workspace-panel__header-1">
        <svg className="panel-workspace-panel__header-1__icon" width={9} height={9} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M1.25 12C1.25 6.06294 6.06294 1.25 12 1.25C17.9371 1.25 22.75 6.06294 22.75 12C22.75 17.9371 17.9371 22.75 12 22.75C6.06294 22.75 1.25 17.9371 1.25 12Z" fill="currentColor" clipRule="evenodd" fillRule="evenodd" strokeWidth="2" />
        </svg>
        <span>3</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 12, padding: "0px 24px" }}>
    <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 12 }}>
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1">
        <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__label">
          <span>Review progress</span>
          <span>60%</span>
        </span>
        <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
          <span className="panel-workspace-panel__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "16px 24px" }}>
    <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" data-fill style={{ gap: 8, alignItems: "stretch" }}>
      <button className="panel-workspace-panel__footer-1" data-flex-item type="button">
        + New item
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>
      </Collapsible.Panel>
      <div className="panel-workspace-panel__hide">
<Collapsible.Trigger className="panel-workspace-panel__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Workspace Panel" /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-workspace-panel__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
  <svg className="panel-workspace-panel__hide-1__icon" width={17} height={17} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
    <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
  </svg>
</button></div>
</div>
    </Collapsible.Root>
  );
}
```

```tsx ProgressPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";

interface ProgressPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
}

/** Primary progress, from the brand kit. */
export function ProgressPrimary({ className }: ProgressPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <div className={className ? `progress-primary ${className}` : "progress-primary"}>
      <span className="progress-primary__label">
        <span>Primary</span>
        <span>60%</span>
      </span>
      <span className="progress-primary__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Primary" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>

      </span>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```css NavbarPrimary.css
.navbar-primary__slots { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }

.navbar-primary__slots[data-dividers] > .navbar-primary__slot + .navbar-primary__slot { border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 14%, transparent); }
.navbar-primary__slot, .navbar-primary__items { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }
.navbar-primary__items { align-items: flex-start; }
.navbar-primary__slot[data-led] { flex-direction: row; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; }
.navbar-primary__slot[data-led] > .navbar-primary__items { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.navbar-primary__items[data-layout="row"] { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; }
.navbar-primary__items[data-layout="grid"] { display: grid; }
.navbar-primary__items[data-fill] > * { align-self: stretch; width: auto; min-width: 0; }
.navbar-primary__items[data-fill][data-layout="row"] > :not([data-flex-item]) { flex: 1 1 0; }
.navbar-primary__slot[data-led] > .navbar-primary__items[data-fill] { flex: 1 1 0; }
.navbar-primary__lead { flex: 1 1 8rem; min-width: 0; }
.navbar-primary__slots { flex-direction: row; align-items: center; }
.navbar-primary__slots:not([data-dividers]) { gap: 0; }
.navbar-primary__slot { flex: 0 0 auto; }
.navbar-primary__slot[data-slot="body"] { flex: 1 1 auto; }
```

```tsx TooltipPrimary.tsx
import type * as React from "react";
import "./TooltipPrimary.css";

interface TooltipPrimaryProps {
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}

/** Primary tooltip, from the brand kit. */
export function TooltipPrimary({ className, children = "Primary" }: TooltipPrimaryProps) {
  return (
    <span className={className ? `tooltip-primary ${className}` : "tooltip-primary"} role="tooltip">
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}
```

## upload · Dropzone

<PreviewUploadDropzone />

<Check>
**Accessibility: no issues found.** axe-core 4.14.0 passed 5 WCAG 2.2 AA checks on this component.
</Check>

<CodeGroup>
```tsx UploadDropzone.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import type * as React from "react";
import "./UploadDropzone.css";

interface UploadDropzoneProps {
  accept?: string;
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  multiple?: boolean;
  onFilesSelected?: (files: File[]) => void;
}

/** Upload dropzone, from the brand kit. */
export function UploadDropzone({
  accept,
  className,
  children = "Dropzone",
  multiple = false,
  onFilesSelected,
}: UploadDropzoneProps) {
  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const selectFiles = (files: File[]) => {
    const selected = multiple ? files : files.slice(0, 1);
    if (selected.length === 0) {
      return;
    }
    setFileNames(selected.map((file) => file.name));
    onFilesSelected?.(selected);
  };
  const handleDragOver = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLLabelElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (event.dataTransfer.types.includes("Files")) {
      setDragging(true);
    }
  };
  const handleDragLeave = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLLabelElement>) => {
    const next = event.relatedTarget;
    if (next instanceof Node && event.currentTarget.contains(next)) {
      return;
    }
    setDragging(false);
  };
  const handleDrop = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLLabelElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setDragging(false);
    selectFiles(Array.from(event.dataTransfer.files));
  };
  const handleChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    selectFiles(Array.from(event.currentTarget.files ?? []));
    event.currentTarget.value = "";
  };

  return (
    <label
      className={["upload-dropzone", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}
      data-dragging={dragging || undefined}
      onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
      onDragOver={handleDragOver}
      onDrop={handleDrop}
    >
      <input
        accept={accept}
        aria-label="Choose file"
        className="upload-dropzone__input"
        multiple={multiple}
        onChange={handleChange}
        type="file"
      />
      <span>{children}</span>
```

## upload · Upload Files

<PreviewUploadFiles />

<Check>
**Accessibility: no issues found.** axe-core 4.14.0 passed 5 WCAG 2.2 AA checks on this component.
</Check>

<CodeGroup>
```tsx UploadFiles.tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import type * as React from "react";
import "./UploadFiles.css";

interface UploadFilesProps {
  accept?: string;
  className?: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  multiple?: boolean;
  onFilesSelected?: (files: File[]) => void;
}

/** Upload dropzone, from the brand kit. */
export function UploadFiles({
  accept,
  className,
  children = "Upload Files",
  multiple = false,
  onFilesSelected,
}: UploadFilesProps) {
  const [fileNames, setFileNames] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);
  const selectFiles = (files: File[]) => {
    const selected = multiple ? files : files.slice(0, 1);
    if (selected.length === 0) {
      return;
    }
    setFileNames(selected.map((file) => file.name));
    onFilesSelected?.(selected);
  };
  const handleDragOver = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLLabelElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (event.dataTransfer.types.includes("Files")) {
      setDragging(true);
    }
  };
  const handleDragLeave = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLLabelElement>) => {
    const next = event.relatedTarget;
    if (next instanceof Node && event.currentTarget.contains(next)) {
      return;
    }
    setDragging(false);
  };
  const handleDrop = (event: React.DragEvent<HTMLLabelElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setDragging(false);
    selectFiles(Array.from(event.dataTransfer.files));
  };
  const handleChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    selectFiles(Array.from(event.currentTarget.files ?? []));
    event.currentTarget.value = "";
  };

  return (
    <label
      className={["upload-files", className].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}
      data-dragging={dragging || undefined}
      onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
      onDragOver={handleDragOver}
      onDrop={handleDrop}
    >
      <input
        accept={accept}
        aria-label="Choose file"
        className="upload-files__input"
        multiple={multiple}
        onChange={handleChange}
        type="file"
      />
      <span>{children}</span>
      {fileNames.length > 0 ? (
        <span aria-live="polite" className="upload-files__file">
          {fileNames.join(", ")}
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </label>
  );
}
```

      <div className="panel-primary__inner" data-panel-open={open} inert={!open}><div className="panel-primary__surface">
      <div className="panel-primary__slots" data-dividers>
        <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="header" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "16px 24px" }}>
          <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "center" }}>
          <strong className="panel-primary__lead panel-primary__heading">Primary</strong>
            <span className="panel-primary__header-1">
              <svg className="panel-primary__header-1__icon" width={9} height={9} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
                <path d="M1.25 12C1.25 6.06294 6.06294 1.25 12 1.25C17.9371 1.25 22.75 6.06294 22.75 12C22.75 17.9371 17.9371 22.75 12 22.75C6.06294 22.75 1.25 17.9371 1.25 12Z" fill="currentColor" clipRule="evenodd" fillRule="evenodd" strokeWidth="2" />
              </svg>
              <span>3</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="body" style={{ gap: 12, padding: "24px 24px" }}>
          <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="column" style={{ gap: 12 }}>
            <div className="panel-primary__body-1">
              <span className="panel-primary__body-1__label">
                <span>Review progress</span>
                <span>60%</span>
              </span>
              <span className="panel-primary__body-1__track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Review progress" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={60}>
                <span className="panel-primary__body-1__fill" style={{ inlineSize: "60%" }} />
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-primary__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "20px 24px" }}>
          <div className="panel-primary__items" data-layout="row" data-fill style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap", alignItems: "stretch" }}>
            <button className="panel-primary__footer-1" type="button">
              + New item
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div></div>
      <div className="panel-primary__hide">
      <button type="button" className="panel-primary__trigger" aria-label="Toggle Primary" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} /><div inert aria-hidden="true"><button className="panel-primary__hide-1" type="button" aria-label="Arrow Left">
        <svg className="panel-primary__hide-1__icon" width={17} height={17} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth="2" />
        </svg>
      </button></div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </div>
);
};

              </svg>

                <span>Review progress</span>
                <span>60%</span>
              </span>

              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="panel-workspace-panel__slot" data-slot="footer" style={{ gap: 8, padding: "16px 24px" }}>
          <div className="panel-workspace-panel__items" data-layout="column" data-fill style={{ gap: 8, alignItems: "stretch" }}>
            <button className="panel-workspace-panel__footer-1" data-flex-item type="button">
              + New item
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div></div>

);
};
